Alfa 147 workshop manual: text version · guide

Component index

Component code (B002 = junction unit under the dashboard) -> its pinout pages, the repair procedures that replace it, and every topic that refers to it. Descriptions in the text sometimes drop the leading zeros (B2 = B002).

  • 2710A32 intermediate drive shaft bearing support - r + r with intermediate drive shaft removed
  • 2710A35 intermediate drive shaft mount - r r
  • 5530A10 alternator - r+r

  • A30D Ignition coil (cyl. 1) ( ENGINE )
  • A30E Ignition coil (cyl. 1) ( ENGINE )
  • A30F Ignition coil (cyl. 1) ( ENGINE )

Replaced by:

  • 5510C coil, ecu, sensors

Referred to in (repair procedure (removal / refitting; the operation number is the code)):

  • 1004E10 engine, removed - remove cylinder head and oil sump for inspection - includes positioning on stand and removal
  • 1016D14 cylinder heads - r + r and replace gaskets with engine removed
  • 1020D17 engind front cover - r r
  • 1024A10 crank shaft - r + r with engine removed - check main and connecting rod bearingand replace if necessary
  • 1056B90 system cable bundle - r + r
  • 1088C20 water pump - r + r with engine removed
  • 5510C14 ignition coil/s - r+r
  • 5510C16 left hand cylinder head ignition coil - r+r
  • 5510C18 right hand cylinder head ignition coil - r+r
